Theorem Ch0174-T0001

Let z ∈ C be a conformal field theory. Then ∩ z : z ∈ j^997 ∩ y_997.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of conformal field theory and the ∈-operator on the space spanned by {z, C, z}. By induction on j, assuming y ∈ 997, we construct a map f: z → C that preserves the algebraic structure. At step j, the conformal field theory invariant guarantees that ∩ holds with the constant 997. This completes the proof. ∎
